  ✔ one model tool use becomes a semantic operation call, before any native execution (28.538375ms)

  ✔ cancellation propagates where the framework supports it (0.737042ms)
  ✔ no callback in this package can reach the gateway, a dispatcher, or a settlement path (35.460542ms)

✔ imported MCP identity is chosen locally (10.68325ms)
